However, existing methods follow a think-first-then-decide paradigm, i.e., they first sample multiple reasoning paths, which inevitably leads to overgeneration, then prune or stop unnecessary paths to compensate. In contrast, decide-first-then-think, i.e., first identifying points that are likely to lead to desirable generations, has been underexplored so far. Followi","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-30T10:59:20Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2606_31484.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是推理时并行采样的一个老问题：为了拿到更好的 reasoning trace，现有 parallel thinking 方法通常一开始就并行采很多条路径，再在中途或末尾做剪枝、投票或聚合。这样做的问题不是思路不对，而是算力花得太早、太粗。大量分支其实在前期就已经偏离有效解空间，后面再剪掉只是补救。作者提出的核心问题是：能不能先用一条 seed path 判断“哪里值得分叉”，再只在这些位置展开并行推理，从而减少无效生成，同时保留多样性带来的收益。","importance":"对基座研究本身，它不是预训练方法论文，但对 post-train 之后的 inference-time scaling 有直接启发，尤其适合关注“推理能力到底来自模型本身，还是来自搜索/采样策略”的读者。它的重要性主要有两点。第一，它把 parallel reasoning 的默认范式从 think-first-then-decide 改成 decide-first-then-think，这个视角是清楚的，也有一定普适性。第二，它提示了一个更一般的问题：模型内部的置信度信号能否作为 test-time compute allocation 的依据。这个问题和动态解码、early exit、adaptive compute、甚至训练时的 curriculum/uncertainty 建模都有接口。贡献量级不算改写范式，但属于一个值得继续追的推理时策略点。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Fork-Think with Confidence，核心流程分三步。第一步，先对问题生成一条 greedy seed path，而不是一开始就采样多条 reasoning trace。第二步，在这条 seed path 上识别适合“分叉”的 token 位置。作者借用了 pivot token 的想法：某些 token 对后续生成轨迹影响很大，如果在这些位置附近改变 continuation，后续 reasoning path 的差异会被放大。具体识别信号来自模型置信度，直觉上是当模型在某个位置不够确定时，这里更可能是后续推理走向分化的关键点。第三步，只在选中的 forking points 上采样多个 continuation，再对这些分支做聚合得到最终答案。\n\n和已有方法相比，差异不在于更复杂的 verifier 或 scorer，而在于把“采样预算”从序列起点后移到更有信息的位置。传统 parallel sampling 在 prompt 之后立刻分叉，优点是简单，缺点是前缀阶段的随机性会制造很多表面不同、实质低效的路径。Fork-Think 先走一条确定性主干，再在局部高不确定位置展开，等于把多样性集中投放到更可能改变结论的步骤上。论文里还强调，这个框架和已有 pruning、aggregation 方法是兼容的，不是替代关系。也就是说，它更像一个前置的 compute allocation 策略。\n\n从机制上看，这个方法成立依赖两个假设：一是 greedy seed path 至少能走到“有代表性”的中间状态；二是 token-level confidence 与后续 reasoning branching value 有相关性。作者的实验基本支持这两个假设，但也意味着它对 seed path 质量有依赖。如果模型一开始就走偏，后续再聪明地分叉也只是围绕错误主干展开。","experiments":"论文在 3 个模型和 3 个 reasoning benchmark 上做了系统评测。摘要和正文给出的主结果是：相较于标准 parallel think，Fork-Think 在保持或提升任务表现的同时，整体 token cost 最多下降约 30%，整体运行时间最多下降约 57%。这个结果说明收益主要来自两部分：一是少生成了无效分支；二是分叉发生得更晚，前缀共享更多，因此真实解码开销下降。\n\n作者还做了几类比较关键的消融。第一类是 forking position 的影响。结论很明确：较晚的分叉点通常比一开始就并行采样效果更好，说明 reasoning 的关键不在“尽早制造多样性”，而在“在合适的位置制造多样性”。第二类是不同 confidence-based 选点策略的比较，用来验证置信度是否真能找到有价值的 pivot。第三类是与不同 aggregation 方式的组合实验，说明 Fork-Think 不是依赖某一种特定投票器才成立。\n\n从阅读价值看，最值得注意的不是单个 benchmark 上的绝对分数，而是 compute-performance 曲线的形状变化：在相近预算下，它能把更多算力放到高价值分支上，因此效率更高。如果正文里给了不同 fork 数、不同 seed decoding 策略、不同温度设置的结果，这些表通常比主表更能说明方法边界。","reading_guide":"泛读建议控制在 20 到 30 分钟。先看 Figure 1，把 think-first-then-decide 和 decide-first-then-think 的差别吃透；这是整篇最重要的 conceptual move。然后看方法部分里 forking point 的定义与 confidence 计算方式，重点确认作者到底用的是 token probability、entropy、margin，还是某种归一化后的不确定性指标。接着看主实验表，关注 token cost、wall-clock time 和 accuracy 三者是否同时报告。最后看消融，尤其是“later fork is better”那组结果，它决定这篇工作的说服力。\n\n如果只想判断是否值得进一步跟进，优先看三处：方法总览图、fork position 消融、与标准 parallel think 的效率对比。若要深挖，可以继续想两个问题：一，confidence 是否只是一个便宜 proxy，未来能否换成 value-of-branch 估计器；二，这种 decide-first-then-think 能否迁移到 tree search、speculative reasoning 或 verifier-guided decoding。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper revisits a basic inefficiency in parallel reasoning at inference time. Most existing methods follow a think-first-then-decide pattern: sample multiple reasoning traces up front, then prune, stop, or aggregate later. This works, but it spends compute too early and too broadly. Many branches are low-value from the start, so later pruning only recovers part of the waste. The paper asks a cleaner question: can we first identify where branching is likely to matter, and only then expand multiple continuations from those points?","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to foundation-model research through the test-time compute lens. Its value is less about raw benchmark gains and more about reframing how inference-time scaling should allocate budget. Two aspects matter. First, it proposes a clear alternative to the default parallel sampling recipe: decide-first-then-think. Second, it suggests that model confidence can be used as a signal for adaptive compute allocation. That connects naturally to broader topics such as uncertainty-aware decoding, adaptive computation, verifier-guided search, and even training-time uncertainty modeling. The contribution is moderate in scale, but the framing is useful.","technical_approach":"$12","experiments":"$13","reading_guide":"$14","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"推理时计算定向分配的简洁有效方法，§3的分叉点识别策略可直接复现到内部推理pipeline，重点看方法和消融实验","en":"Simple and effective method for targeted test-time compute allocation; §3 forking point identification is directly reproducible for internal reasoning pipelines, focus on method and ablation"},"metadata":{"institutions":["Saarland Informatics Campus","Saarland University","Saarland Informatics Campus, Saarland University"],"authors":["Zena Al-Khalili","Rafi Hakim","Dietrich Klakow","Ji-Ung Lee"],"notable_authors":["Dietrich Klakow"],"tags":["test-time compute","reasoning enhancement","inference efficiency","self-consistency","model confidence"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31484","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.31484","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.30852","title":"When Does Learning to Stop Help? A stronger model can look better calibrated partly because it makes fewer mistakes. This can lead to ranking reversals after accuracy is controlled.","importance":"The main value is in evaluation methodology rather than pretraining itself. It is relevant for comparing base models, post-trained models, reasoning models, and different confidence elicitation schemes. The result cautions against treating raw calibration metrics as directly comparable across models with different task accuracies.","technical_approach":"The proposed framework is ACE, or Accuracy-Controlled Evaluation. It contains three complementary views. Instance-Aligned calibration compares models only on examples where they share the same correctness outcome, giving strict instance-level control. Distribution-Aligned calibration matches the aggregate mixture of correct and incorrect cases, keeping broader coverage while relaxing instance matching. Candidate-Aligned calibration applies the same idea when models evaluate a shared candidate answer pool. The method does not recalibrate models; it changes the comparison protocol so that calibration behavior is less entangled with accuracy.","experiments":"The empirical study spans GPQA, LiveBench, MMLU, FreshQA, TriviaQA, multiple model families, and several confidence elicitation methods. A central result is that raw ECE differences are strongly correlated with accuracy differences across model pairs: Figure 1 reports Spearman ρ=-0.670 over n=40 dataset-level comparisons, with 95% CI [-0.81, -0.45] and p<1e-5. After accuracy control, the correlation is much weaker for Distribution-Aligned calibration, ρ=-0.232 and not significant, and for Instance-Aligned calibration, ρ=-0.174 and not significant. Candidate-Aligned calibration still shows a moderate residual relation, ρ=-0.454. Lean 的“陈述形式化”任务里，Lean 编译通过只能说明类型/语法正确，不能保证语义忠实。论文要解决的是：如何在没有形式等价证明的前提下，规模化评估“编译通过但语义跑偏”的失败，并把失败归因到常见 pipeline 组件（起草模型、Mathlib/上下文检索、elaboration pretrain、或用形式化数据做持续预训练的人，这个工作提供了更可靠的过滤信号：仅用编译/类型检查做数据筛选会系统性放进大量语义错误样本，污染后续预训练或 mid-train。","technical_approach":"方法核心是把 statement formalization 的成功拆成两层：\n1) Validity：Lean 编译/类型检查是否通过（必要条件）。\n2) Faithfulness：生成的 Lean 声明是否与原自然语言陈述语义一致（关键但不可由 Lean 内核判定）。\n\n为评估 faithfulness，论文采用“交叉模型语义裁判 + 严格共识 + 人类专家校准”的协议：用多个独立 LLM judge 对自然语言陈述与生成的 Lean 声明做语义一致性判断，报告共识通过率，并用数学专家抽检来估计该共识标签的精度/保守性。作者强调这不是形式等价证明，而是一个高精度的语义过滤 operating point：共识 positive 更可信；共识 negative（尤其是 compile-pass 但 unfaithful）大多是真语义错误。\n\n在系统归因上，论文用 factorial ablation 把常见工具链拆成三条轴：\n- Parametric drafting：直接由模型起草 Lean 声明（可能包含专门 formalizer 或通用 LLM）。\n- Mathlib/context grounding：检索/提供相关定义、定理、命名空间与上下文，减少“写对 Lean 但写错对象”的空间。\n- Lean elaboration feedback：利用 elaborator/报错信息做迭代修复，主要提升 validity。\n\n关键点不只是“反馈有用”，而是区分：哪些组件主要修复 validity，哪些真正提升 faithfulness，哪些只是降低修复成本/提高效率但不改变语义错误率。","experiments":"基准：400 条研究生水平陈述，覆盖 real analysis / complex analysis / topology / algebra，来源于公开数学材料并人工整理。\n\n核心结果：\n- 一个“全工具增强 agent”能达到 89.5% 的 compilation（validity）通过率，但在语义共识 faithfulness 上只有 60.5%。也就是存在显著的 compile-pass 但语义不忠实的桶，规模接近三分之一。\n- 人类专家校准显示：共识 positive 的精度较高；而对 compile-pass 但共识 negative 的样本，人工复核通常确认是实质语义偏差（如漏假设、换域、把非平凡命题写成 vacuous/弱化版本等）。\n\n归因结论（定性但很关键）：\n- Elaboration feedback 对 validity 修复贡献最大。\n- Search/grounding 更影响“写到正确的数学对象/定义”与效率，和 faithfulness 更相关。\n- 在已有 grounding + feedback 的工具栈下，单纯更强的 parametric drafting（含专门微调）对最终 faithfulness 的边际收益变小，更多表现为减少修复轮次或提升初稿质量，而不是根治语义漂移。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 30–45 分钟）：\n- 重点看 Introduction 里对“compile 不是语义正确”的失败模式拆解，以及他们为什么选择“跨模型共识 + 人类校准”的保守指标。\n- 看 benchmark 构建与任务定义部分：400 条陈述的学科分布、难度定位、输入输出格式约束。\n- 看 evaluation protocol：judge 设计、共识规则、以及人类校准如何验证 precision（这是整篇可信度的支点）。\n- 看 factorial ablation 的结果图表：分别对 validity / faithfulness / efficiency 的贡献拆分，理解作者“哪些组件在修什么”的结论依据。\n\n深挖点：如果你在做形式化数据合成用于预训练，建议把他们的“compile-pass but unfaithful”案例当作负样本类型库，反推更强的数据过滤/对比学习信号，而不是继续用 compile 作为唯一 gate。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In NL-to-Lean statement formalization, compilation/type-checking is only a validity filter, not a semantic guarantee. The paper tackles how to evaluate *faithful* formalization at scale—detecting compile-pass but meaning-shifted Lean declarations—and how to attribute failures/gains to common pipeline components (drafting model, Mathlib/context grounding, and Lean elaboration feedback).","importance":"This is primarily an evaluation and bottleneck-attribution contribution rather than a new pretraining recipe. For researchers building Lean/math corpora for pretraining or continual pretraining, it provides a concrete warning and a calibrated protocol: filtering by compilation alone will systematically admit a large fraction of semantically wrong statements, which can poison downstream training. It also offers a more diagnostic lens than compile-rate for comparing tool-augmented systems.","technical_approach":"$15","experiments":"Benchmark: 400 graduate-level statements across real analysis, complex analysis, topology, and algebra.\n\nHeadline numbers: a full tool-augmented agent achieves 89.5% compilation but only 60.5% consensus faithfulness, revealing a large compile-pass/meaning-failure regime.\n\nHuman calibration: consensus-positive labels have high precision; many audited compile-pass but consensus-negative cases are confirmed as genuine semantic errors (missing hypotheses, domain shifts, vacuous/weakened claims, etc.).\n\nAttribution findings (high-level): elaboration feedback dominates validity repair; grounding/search contributes more to correct semantic targeting and efficiency; once grounding + feedback are present, stronger parametric drafting becomes more redundant for final faithfulness (more about cheaper trajectories than fixing semantic drift).","reading_guide":"Skim plan (30–45 minutes):\n- Read the Introduction for the precise failure taxonomy of compile-pass but unfaithful statements and the motivation for consensus judging.\n- Focus on the evaluation protocol section: judge setup, consensus rule, and the human audit methodology (this is the credibility anchor).\n- Inspect the main results table contrasting compilation vs faithfulness.\n- Study the factorial ablation figures/tables that decompose gains into validity vs faithfulness vs efficiency.\n\nIf you build formal-math data for training, pay special attention to the confirmed failure cases—they are actionable templates for better filtering and negative sampling.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不是在提更强模型，而是在拆穿一个坏指标。做 formalization、tool use 或 judge 设计的人都该看那 29-point gap 和人工校准细节。","en":"This paper matters because it breaks a bad metric rather than proposing a stronger model. UCF101-AD targets action denial: when the context strongly suggests an action but the action itself is absent, can the model correctly reject the premise?","importance":"The contribution is mainly a VLM evaluation and data-diagnostic asset. If models are trained and evaluated mostly on positive action labels, they can learn scene-action or object-action correlations instead of temporal verification. For video-language pretraining, hard negatives and counterfactual clips are not just extra data; they provide supervision that forces the representation to distinguish contextual evidence from the motion that defines the event.","technical_approach":"UCF101-AD is built from UCF101 action categories and pairs Action-Presence clips with Action-Denial clips. A denial example preserves the typical actors, objects, locations, and co-occurring motion, while removing the defining motion of the target action. For example, a clip may contain a basketball court, players, the ball, and dribbling, but no dunk. The proposed CausalAct framework represents entities, relations, scene conditions, and action preconditions as natural-language causal graphs. Models are then prompted to verify whether the necessary conditions for the target action hold. We ask how closely LLMs' belief updates match those of ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-29T19:30:32Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_30850.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有大模型评测多为单轮设置且仅关注最终答案准确性，无法衡量多轮证据逐步积累场景下模型的信念更新过程是否符合理性，也缺乏可控的专用基准来直接评测多轮信念更新能力。","importance":"提供了专门的多轮证据整合与信念校准评测基准，可用于评估预训练/后训练对模型多轮推理与不确定性校准能力的影响；模型隐变量推断能力与下游预测校准能力脱节的发现，为相关训练目标的设计提供了具体参考。","technical_approach":"借鉴认知心理学的书包-扑克筹码研究范式，构建BayesBench基准套件，包含三层复杂度递增的贝叶斯任务，对应四个评测环境：贝叶斯估计（抛硬币，估计未知硬币偏差）、贝叶斯预测（推荐系统，推断用户类型并预测留存电影评分）、隐框架贝叶斯预测（社会判断/Reddit裁决、医疗分诊，需同时推断隐状态与证据的语义框架/沟通风格）。基准逐轮评测模型的信念轨迹，有解析解的任务与贝叶斯参考后验对比，下游预测任务与真实结果对比，还设置主动交互与被动观察的对照以检测交互带来的偏差。","experiments":"在7个3B到70B参数量的开源LLM上评测，发现三个稳定规律：一是模型能从累积证据中推断隐结构，规模提升会增强推断能力，大模型更易区分硬币偏差、准确识别用户类型、可靠推断患者沟通风格；二是更好的隐变量推断未转化为校准的下游预测，大模型在抛硬币任务中会过度更新，将预测推向极端，推荐系统和医疗分诊中显式基于推断隐变量条件化能提升性能，但模型仍无法可靠将推断结果转化为准确预测，分诊场景下倾向把中等紧急度标签推向极端；三是主动交互会偏置证据积累，社会判断任务中主动对话相比被动观察相同证据存在持续的亲用户偏差，医疗分诊中主动交互会降低紧急度评估准确性。","reading_guide":"泛读重点看引言中多轮信念更新评测的缺口分析，第2节的三层贝叶斯任务设计逻辑与四个环境的结构，以及引言和结果部分总结的三个核心发现。预计阅读时间25-35分钟，无需深究单个环境的具体数据生成流程细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Most existing LLM evaluations are single-turn and only measure final answer accuracy, so they cannot assess whether a model's belief updating process is rational as evidence accumulates across multiple turns, and there is a lack of controlled dedicated benchmarks for directly measuring multi-turn belief updating capabilities.","importance":"Provides a dedicated benchmark for multi-turn evidence integration and belief calibration, which can be used to evaluate the impact of pretraining and post-training on models' multi-turn reasoning and uncertainty calibration capabilities. The finding that latent inference capability does not translate to calibrated downstream prediction offers concrete reference for the design of related training objectives.","technical_approach":"Presents BayesBench, a benchmark suite adapted from the classic bookbag-and-poker-chip cognitive psychology paradigm, organized around three Bayesian tasks of increasing complexity instantiated in four environments: Bayesian estimation (coin flip, estimating unknown coin bias), Bayesian prediction (recommender system, inferring user type from ratings and predicting held-out movie ratings), and latent-framed Bayesian prediction (social judgment / Reddit verdicts and medical triage, requiring joint inference of latent state and semantic framing / communication style of evidence). The benchmark scores per-turn belief trajectories against closed-form Bayesian posteriors where available, and downstream predictions against ground-truth outcomes. It also includes active interaction and passive observation settings to measure interaction-induced biases.","experiments":"Evaluates seven open-weight LLMs (3B–70B parameters) and identifies three consistent patterns: First, models infer latent structure from accumulating evidence, and scaling improves this capability — larger models better separate coin biases, identify user types more accurately, and infer patient communication styles more reliably. Second, better latent inference does not translate to calibrated downstream prediction — larger models over-update in the coin task, pushing predictions to extremes, explicit conditioning on inferred latents improves performance in recommendation and triage tasks but models still fail to reliably translate inferred knowledge into accurate predictions, and models tend to push middle-urgency labels to extremes in triage. Third, active engagement biases evidence accumulation — active conversation introduces a persistent pro-user bias relative to passive observation of the same evidence in social judgment, and active engagement degrades urgency assessment accuracy in medical triage.","reading_guide":"Skim the Introduction for the gap analysis of multi-turn belief updating evaluation. We formalize the LLM Jury under the Huber contamination model and show that PoLL incurs unbounded bias   under any positive contamination, regardless of jury size, whenever a single judge fails in a biased, LLM-typical way (mode collapse, sycophancy, safety refusal). GM satisfies three core properties simultaneously: no tuning hyperparameters (no need to pre-set contamination rate), preserves joint cross-attribute distance structure, and achieves the optimal 1/2 breakdown point. It compares four conditions: baseline repair without explicit localization, repair guided by Qwen4B-predicted file hints, repair guided by Gemma4E4B-predicted file hints, and repair guided by gold modified-file sets derived from benchmark references. The localization stage uses a similar agent scaffold with read-only constraints and returns a structured list of files. The repair stage follows a mini-SWE-agent-style loop that can inspect files, edit the workspace, execute commands, and submit a patch. The key design choice is controlled intervention: keep the repair backbone fixed and vary only the upstream file hints.","experiments":"The study evaluates three repair backbones on SWE-bench Verified. In these settings, outcome-only rewards provide too sparse guidance, failing to inform the model about the goodness of intermediate actions. Dense supervision methods aim to solve this problem by scoring intermediate steps, from intrinsic confidence to self-distillation and embedding similarities. However, it is common practice to evaluate them by measuring the downstream perfo","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-30T17:58:23Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_32034.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"长程 LLM Agent 的训练需要密集监督信号，但现有做法通常把信号接入完整 RL 或后训练流程后再看最终收益。这种评估成本高，而且把信号质量、RL 算法、归一化、loss 接法、优化细节等因素混在一起。QVal 试图回答一个更直接的问题：不做训练，能否先判断某个中间步骤打分信号是否接近真实 action value。","importance":"这项工作对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 pretrain 到 posttrain 的接口有参考价值。长程 Agent 训练的瓶颈之一是信用分配，尤其是几百到几千步轨迹中，结果奖励很难告诉模型哪一步有用。QVal 提供了一个低成本筛选 dense supervision 的协议，可以在昂贵训练前排除质量较差的信号。更重要的是，它把 dense reward、value signal、test-time search heuristic 放到同一个 Q-value 对齐框架下比较，减少了只看最终训练曲线带来的误判。","technical_approach":"核心做法是构造由 state-action pair 组成的数据集，每个样本标注一个 reference Q-value，即从该状态动作继续执行、在某个专家参考策略下的期望回报。专家策略在简单环境中可以来自最优策略；在开放环境中则用较强模型的多次 rollout 估计，通常取较优轨迹形成参考。被评估的方法只需要对同一批 state-action pair 打分，然后计算 Q-alignment，也就是这些分数能否按 reference Q-value 正确排序。这样，直接 prompting、intrinsic confidence、自蒸馏、embedding similarity、代码生成式信号、ranking predictor、预训练模型打分等不同来源的 dense signal 可以在同一输入、同一目标、同一上下文下比较。","experiments":"QVal-v1.0 覆盖四类多轮环境：TerminalBench 的编程和终端交互、OpenApps 的图形界面和应用操作、ALFWorld 的具身文本环境、FrozenLake 的目标导航。实验评估了 21 种 dense supervision 方法，分为七个方法族：direct prompting、intrinsic signals、code generation、self-distillation、ranking-based prediction、pre-trained models、embedding similarity。主要结果是 direct prompting 和 ranking 方法平均 Q-alignment 更强；同一方法族内增加复杂度通常没有明显收益；code-based 方法在较小、结构化环境中表现较好，但在更开放的任务上变弱；文本观测比图像观测更容易得到稳定对齐；action-value 和 state-value 两类目标下的方法相对排序大体保持一致。结果也说明环境难度不能用单一排序解释，不同方法族会受状态空间、动作空间、模态和反馈形式不同影响。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25 到 35 分钟。先看引言和 Figure 1，确认 QVal 如何把 dense supervision 转成 Q-value 排序问题。再看方法部分中 reference Q-value 的构造方式，重点判断专家策略和 best-of-rollouts 会引入什么偏差。实验部分建议重点看 21 种方法的分组结果、不同环境上的排名变化、文本与图像观测差异、action-value 与 state-value 的对照。若关心 Agent RL 训练信号，最值得深挖的是 Q-alignment 与真实后训练收益之间未来如何校准；这决定它能否成为训练前筛选指标，而不只是一个离线诊断工具。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Long-horizon LLM agents need dense supervision, but most methods are evaluated only after being integrated into a full RL or post-training pipeline. That makes evaluation expensive and hard to interpret, because the measured gain mixes the quality of the signal with algorithmic details, normalization, loss integration, optimization choices, and other engineering factors. QVal asks a narrower question: before running training, can a dense signal be tested for whether it orders intermediate actions like a reference action-value function would?","importance":"The contribution is mainly about the post-training interface rather than base pretraining. It is still relevant to foundation-model research because long-horizon agent training is increasingly limited by credit assignment. Outcome-only rewards are weak when trajectories contain hundreds or thousands of actions. QVal gives a cheap filter for candidate value signals before expensive RL runs. It also places reward shaping, dense value estimation, self-distilled feedback, and test-time search heuristics under a common Q-value alignment view.","technical_approach":"QVal builds datasets of state-action pairs. Each pair receives a reference Q-value: the expected return after taking that action and continuing under an expert reference policy. In simple environments, the expert can be an optimal policy; in more open-ended environments, the reference is estimated from rollouts of a strong model, often using the best among several continuations. A candidate dense supervision method then scores the same state-action pairs. The metric, Q-alignment, measures how well the candidate scores rank samples relative to the reference Q-values. This design allows direct prompting, intrinsic confidence, self-distillation, embedding similarity, code-based signals, ranking predictors, and pretrained-model-based scoring to be compared with the same inputs and targets.","experiments":"QVal-v1.0 covers four multi-turn environments: TerminalBench for programming and terminal interaction, OpenApps for computer and application use, ALFWorld for embodied reasoning, and FrozenLake for navigation. The study evaluates 21 dense supervision methods across seven families: direct prompting, intrinsic signals, code generation, self-distillation, ranking-based prediction, pretrained models, and embedding similarity. Direct prompting and ranking methods perform best on average. Code-based methods are strong in smaller and more structured environments, but degrade in more open-ended settings. Added complexity within a family rarely improves alignment over simpler variants. Text observations generally yield stronger alignment than image observations. The relative ordering of methods is largely preserved between action-value and state-value targets, while no single notion of environment difficulty explains all results.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25 to 35 minutes. Start with the introduction and Figure 1 to understand the reduction from dense supervision evaluation to Q-value ranking. Then read the method section on how reference Q-values are constructed, especially the use of optimal policies versus strong-model rollouts. In the experiments, focus on the family-level comparison of 21 methods, the environment-wise rank changes, the text-versus-image observation gap, and the action-value versus state-value comparison. The main point to examine critically is whether Q-alignment will correlate with actual post-training gains; that determines whether QVal becomes a practical pre-RL screening tool or remains an offline diagnostic benchmark.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"做长horizon Agent RL/密集监督的研究员必看，Q对齐性的设计可直接复用在内部监督信号的筛选与优化中","en":"A must-read for researchers working on long-horizon agent RL or dense supervision; the Q-alignment design can be directly reused for internal supervision signal screening and optimization"},"metadata":{"institutions":["图宾根AI中心","图宾根大学","Tübingen AI Center, University of Tübingen"],"authors":["Sergio Hernández-Gutiérrez","Matteo Merler","Ilze Amanda Auzina","Joschka Strüber","Ameya Prabhu","Matthias Bethge"],"notable_authors":["Matthias Bethge"],"tags":["llm-agent","dense-supervision","evaluation-protocol","long-horizon","reinforcement-learning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32034","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.32034","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.31154","title":"PPT-Eval: A Benchmark for Computer-Use Agents on PowerPoint Tasks","abstract":"Creating and editing slides is a rich, multimodal activity that is ubiquitous in professional and educational settings, making it an ideal testbed for real-world computer-use agents. modality","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-30T08:29:29Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2606_31326.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个统一建模里的老问题在视频域的具体版本：视频理解需要紧凑、判别性的语义表征，视频生成需要高保真、时序连续的密集信号，这两类目标往往把模型推向不同表示空间。纯 AR 视频建模通常受限于视频 token 数量过大、时间冗余高、像素级离散化信噪比低；而把 MLLM 接到 diffusion decoder 后面虽然能做生成，但理解和生成仍是松耦合的两套系统。作者想做的是一个真正共享表示、同时支持视频理解和视频生成的统一框架。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的启发主要有两点。第一，它延续了近两年多模态统一建模的一条重要路线：不要在像素 token 上硬做统一，而是先找到一个更接近语义层、同时又能被生成器消费的共享视觉词表，再让 AR 和 diffusion 各做自己擅长的部分。第二，它把“生成作为理解的辅助监督”明确写进训练目标，而不是只把生成模块当作下游附属头。这对 native multimodal pretrain 很有参考价值，尤其是视频这种高冗余模态：如果共享 token 设计得对，生成损失不只是为了出视频，也能反过来约束理解表征。论文的价值更多在范式和系统设计，而不是某一个单点技巧。","technical_approach":"Vega 的核心设计是 shared vocabulary + hybrid AR-diffusion。文本和视觉都被映射到统一的离散 token 空间，但视频生成不是直接在像素级 token 上做长序列 AR，而是在语义视觉 token 上做自回归，预测关键帧级别的高层语义状态；随后再由 diffusion decoder 把这些语义表示渲染成连续视频。这样分工的原因很明确：AR 更适合建模离散、高层、带因果结构的状态转移，diffusion 更适合补足局部纹理、运动连续性和像素细节。如果把两者都压到同一层级，要么 AR 序列过长、训练困难，要么 diffusion 缺少足够稳定的语义锚点。为了让语义 token 真正成为理解和生成之间的桥，作者强调两点。其一，语义 token 比传统 VQ 像素 token 更紧凑，能显著减少视频 token 数量，缓解时间冗余。其二，这些 token 与文本 token 处在更可对齐的语义层级，因此更适合统一建模。针对生成侧，作者采用 video-centric formulation，把多种任务都写成视频生成问题，连图像编辑也被视为两帧视频的特例。考虑到纯语义 token 会丢失细节，文中加入 noise-controlled visual conditioning，用受控噪声的视觉条件来保留外观细节并增强时序一致性。针对理解侧，作者引入 dual-flow selection 机制，灵感来自 slowfast。它把视觉 token 压缩成两条流：detail flow 用更高压缩率关注快速变化的局部细节，pivot flow 用较低压缩率保留慢变化、结构性更强的关键时刻信息。这样做不是简单降采样，而是按时间尺度分工，减少 token 数量的同时尽量保留对理解有用的动态信息。另一个值得注意的点是 generative visual loss 被当作理解训练的辅助监督，这意味着理解分支不只靠判别式目标学习，还被要求维持一种可生成、可还原的视觉语义结构。与很多“统一”工作相比，Vega 不是把一个冻结的理解模型接到生成器上，而是试图让共享 token、AR 语义建模、diffusion 渲染、理解压缩机制在同一训练框架里协同工作。","experiments":"从全文可见的结果描述看，实验重点是验证三件事。第一，统一框架是否能同时覆盖视频生成和视频理解，而不是一边明显拖后腿。作者声称 Vega 在多种视频生成与理解任务上都取得了有竞争力的表现，并特别强调 image editing 可以自然纳入同一视频中心建模框架。第二，语义空间 AR 相比像素级离散 AR 是否更适合视频。文中论证依据主要是 token 数量显著下降、时间冗余更低，以及 diffusion decoder 能把高层语义预测补全成更高保真、更时序一致的视频。第三，生成监督是否真的帮助理解。作者明确指出 generative visual loss 为视频理解提供了额外学习信号，实验上应有相应消融来显示加入该辅助损失后理解性能提升。另一个关键消融应围绕 dual-flow selection：比较单流压缩、均匀采样、不同压缩率设置，验证 pivot/detail 两条流在保留多时间尺度动态信息上的作用。由于给出的全文片段没有完整表格和具体数值，这里无法可靠复述参数规模、训练数据量和精确提升幅度；但从论文结构判断，最值得看的不是单一 benchmark 分数，而是几组消融：语义 token vs 像素 token、AR-only vs hybrid、是否加入 noise-controlled conditioning、是否加入 generative visual loss、以及 dual-flow 的设计收益。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着统一建模问题去泛读。先看引言和方法总览，抓住作者到底统一了什么：不是把所有模态都塞进一个 AR 解码器，而是把语义层的状态转移交给 AR，把像素层渲染交给 diffusion。然后重点看方法部分关于 shared vocabulary、semantic vision token、dual-flow selection、noise-controlled visual conditioning 的定义和信息流。阅读时要不断问两个问题：为什么视频不能直接照搬图像 unified modeling；为什么这里选择“语义 AR + 像素 diffusion”，而不是纯 AR 或纯 diffusion。实验部分优先看消融，而不是主表排名。尤其关注生成损失是否真的改善理解、dual-flow 是否比简单 token 压缩更有效、以及 image editing 作为两帧视频这一建模统一是否带来额外好处。如果只是判断是否值得后续跟进，30 分钟左右足够；如果要评估它对多模态预训练的借鉴意义，建议细看方法图、token 流程图和消融表。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a central obstacle in unified video modeling: video understanding prefers compact, discriminative semantic representations, while video generation needs dense signals that preserve appearance details and temporal coherence. Existing approaches usually pick one side. Fully autoregressive video modeling struggles with long token sequences, low-SNR pixel tokenization, and heavy temporal redundancy. Existing text-only latent reasoning methods do not adapt to multimodal scenarios, while latent visual reasoning schemes requiring extra image annotations incur high labeling costs and poor scalability.","importance":"Provides a latent reasoning paradigm for multimodal models without additional image annotations, validates the feasibility of replacing long text CoT with extremely short latent state chains, and offers practical reference for reasoning efficiency optimization and reasoning paradigm design of multimodal base models.","technical_approach":"Proposes the CoLT framework, which replaces text reasoning steps with continuous latent representations, and regularizes latent space semantics via three complementary supervision signals: 1) Forward decoding supervision: a lightweight decoder predicts next-step textual reasoning content from preceding latent thoughts to ensure semantic validity and progressiveness of latent representations; 2) Backward decoding supervision: hidden states generated by the decoder from preceding textual thoughts are aligned with latent thought representations to anchor the semantic structure of the latent space; 3) Internal supervision: the model predicts the next-step latent representation from the current latent step to enforce coherent transitions between consecutive latent steps.","experiments":"Evaluated on 8 multimodal benchmarks, outperforms both existing latent reasoning methods and latent visual reasoning schemes relying on image supervision. Compared with text CoT, reduces total inference latency by 10.1× and text decoding latency by 22.6×, requiring only 3 latent thought steps to complete reasoning. Visualizations confirm that effective reasoning patterns are encoded within latent representations.","reading_guide":"Focus on the three-fold supervision mechanism design in Method Chapter 3 (Sections 3.3-3.4), as well as the efficiency comparison experiments and latent representation visualization results in Chapter 4. In this paper, we identify an internal signature of hallucination: progressive degradation of text-to-image cross-attention during generation, leading to specific failure patterns like unfocused or biased attention. Existing mitigation strategies are largely outcome-driven and do not explicitly target this failure mode. To address this problem, we propose AD","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-30T02:46:10Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2606_31054.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.MM"],"summary":{"core_problem":"多模态大模型生成长文本时容易“越写越不看图”，导致后段幻觉。论文要解决的是：能否找到一个可操作的内部信号来刻画这种退化，并在推理与偏好微调阶段直接对 cross-attention 动态做约束，从而提升图像一致性。","importance":"对基座预训练的直接关联中等偏弱，更像是 VLM/MLLM 的对齐与推理时干预方法。但它提出的“cross-attention 动态退化”诊断与“用早期 grounded attention 做锚点”的思路，对做多模态统一建模的人有两点启发：\n- 训练信号不必只在输出层（token loss / preference loss），也可以对中间对齐机制（cross-attn）施加结构化约束。\n- 长输出场景下的 grounding 退化可以被当作一种可度量的训练/推理动态问题，而不是单纯的样本偏好问题。","technical_approach":"ADAPT 把幻觉归因到生成过程中的两阶段转移：早期“视觉扎根阶段”cross-attn 会聚焦到与文本相关的视觉证据；后期进入“语言先验主导阶段”，cross-attn 逐步退化。\n\n作者总结了三类退化信号：\n1) 注意力漂移：从相关区域漂到无关区域，或变得过于发散/空间偏置。\n2) 注意力对新 token 的响应变弱：随生成推进，cross-attn 的变化率下降。\n3) 视觉 token 权重下降：跨层/跨位置对视觉 token 的总体注意力值降低。\n\n在此基础上，ADAPT 由三段组成：\n- Visual Enhance：从早期 decoding 提取“可靠的、语义相关的”多层 cross-attn，并用若干准则做 refinement（文中提到包含位置偏置校正），得到 cross-attn anchor。\n- Attention-Supervised Inference：推理时监控后期 cross-attn 与 anchor 的偏离，当出现 unfocused/biased 等退化模式时做稀疏的纠偏 steering（属于 inference-time 干预）。\n- Visual Attention Guidance DPO：做偏好微调时构造“不同视觉证据条件”的 chosen/rejected 对（anchor-enhanced image 作为 chosen，noise image 作为 rejected），把偏好学习信号与“依赖视觉证据”的注意力模式绑定。\n\n与常见方法的区别：不是只在 logits 上做惩罚或只用 DPO/RLHF 约束输出，而是把 cross-attn 动态当作可监督对象，并把推理时控制与训练时偏好对齐耦合起来。","experiments":"全文片段未给出具体 benchmark 名称与数值表格细节，但作者声称：\n- 在多个基座/骨干 MLLM 上，ADAPT 相比强基线在多项 hallucination/faithfulness 评测上稳定提升。\n- 通过可视化与统计（如注意力变化率、视觉 token 注意力占比随层/随时间的曲线）验证了“后期退化”现象，并展示纠偏后注意力更稳定。\n\n如果要判断方法是否扎实，建议在全文中核对三点：\n1) anchor 的构造是否对 prompt/解码策略鲁棒，是否会把早期错误也固化成锚。\n2) inference-time steering 的代价（额外算力、是否需要回看多层注意力）与对输出流畅性的副作用。\n3) DPO 数据构造是否会引入捷径（模型学会识别 noise image 的分布差异，而非真正增强 grounding）。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 25–35 分钟）：\n- 先看 Cross-Attention and It separates language-side answer stability from visual-evidence reliability, which matters for native multimodal pretraining, visual grounding, and calibration analysis. A VLM can look confident at the output level while its visual input remains ambiguous, so output entropy alone is a weak proxy for multimodal uncertainty.","technical_approach":"The proposed method, Visual Semantic Entropy, perturbs only the image while keeping the text query fixed. It then collects generated answers, clusters semantically equivalent variants into prototypes, and computes uncertainty as a frequency-weighted pairwise distance among prototype embeddings. Compared with Semantic Entropy, it probes instability through visual perturbations rather than decoding noise alone. Compared with SNNE-style text-distance aggregation, it reduces the effect of wording variation through prototype clustering. Compared with paraphrase-based or joint image-text perturbation methods, it avoids conflating prompt sensitivity with visual ambiguity.","experiments":"The evaluation covers 5 VLMs and 5 datasets, with comparisons against sampling-based entropy, semantic-distance methods, text perturbation, and joint image-text perturbation baselines. The diagnostic analyses also support three claims: overconfident visual embeddings suppress output diversity, raw text-embedding distances can overestimate disagreement due to wording changes, and paraphrased prompts often dominate the variability induced by perturbation methods. caution","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-30T12:33:19Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_31575.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR，以GRPO为代表）训练中，两类主流token重要性判断方法存在经验上的矛盾：一类主张仅在高熵token位置更新（80/20规则），另一类主张压制低概率采样token的梯度以避免训练不稳定，但两类方法都能取得实证收益，说明单独使用位置熵或采样token概率都无法准确识别能提供有效学习信号的token，缺少一个有理论依据的统一token级重要性度量。","importance":"这篇工作打通了RLVR中token选择领域两个看似冲突的经验结论，提出的相对惊奇指数（RSI）从token梯度模长与模型不确定性的关系推导而来，不是启发式设计，在多个模型尺寸上稳定提升RLVR训练效率和最终推理性能。其对token级训练信号的分析，既可以直接优化后训练流程，也为预训练阶段的token级损失加权、数据筛选提供了可迁移的思路。","technical_approach":"首先区分了两个容易混淆的信号：位置预测熵代表模型在该位置的整体不确定性，采样token概率代表当前生成的具体token在该位置的似然，两者相关但不等价。高熵位置既可能产生高概率token也可能产生低概率token；低概率token既可能出现在高熵位置（模型不确定，属于有效学习信号），也可能出现在低熵位置（模型高度确定但采样出离群值，会带来不稳定噪声梯度）。在此基础上提出相对惊奇指数（RSI），同时融合位置熵和采样token的相对概率，度量实际采样到的token相对于当前位置预测分布的意外程度。在温和假设下，RSI与token级策略梯度的模长存在解析关联，可以作为token重要性的直接代理。基于RSI设计的token选择方案（RSI-S）会过滤两类无效token：低熵位置的低惊奇token（模型已经掌握，梯度接近零）、低熵位置的高惊奇token（离群采样，梯度不稳定），仅保留能提供稳定有效梯度的token参与更新。","experiments":"在AIME、AMC数学推理基准上，基于Qwen2.5-1.5B、3B、7B三个尺寸的模型，RSI-S的avg@32准确率相对原生GRPO提升2-3个百分点，同时生成的回复长度更短；性能稳定优于仅基于熵的80/20规则和仅基于概率的梯度稳定方法，增益跨模型尺寸一致。消融实验验证，增益来自RSI对熵和采样概率两个信号的融合，单独使用任意一个信号的效果都弱于组合方案。","reading_guide":"重点精读三个部分：一是引言和相关工作中对两类现有token选择方法矛盾的分析，理清位置熵、采样token概率、梯度模长三者的区别与关联；二是RSI的理论推导部分，理解它如何从token级策略梯度表达式出发，统一两类看似冲突的经验结论；三是消融实验部分，观察不同RSI阈值下的训练动态，以及被过滤token的梯度分布特征。值得深挖的方向是，RSI式的token过滤思路能否迁移到预训练阶段，用于token级损失加权或数据筛选，同时过滤模型已经掌握的简单token和噪声离群的无效token。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR, exemplified by GRPO), two dominant token selection heuristics present an apparent empirical contradiction: one line of work prioritizes updates on high-entropy token positions (the 80/20 rule), while the other argues low-probability sampled tokens destabilize gradients and should be downweighted. Both approaches deliver measurable gains, indicating that neither position-level entropy nor sampled token probability alone is sufficient to identify tokens that provide useful learning signal. A principled, unified metric for token-level importance in RLVR is missing.","importance":"This work resolves the apparent tension between entropy-based and probability-based token selection heuristics for RLVR. Standard GRPO uses the final verifier outcome as a uniform advantage over all action tokens. This outcome signal is useful but structurally incomplete: it punishes useful exploration in failed rollouts and reinforces redundant or regressive actions in successful rollouts. We propose TRIAGE, a role-typed credit assignment framework t","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-30T17:48:07Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_32017.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"TRIAGE 处理的是 agentic RL 里的 credit assignment：最终 verifier 只告诉一条轨迹成败，但 GRPO 会把同一个 outcome advantage 均匀分给所有动作 token 或动作片段。这会压制失败轨迹中的有效探索，也会奖励成功轨迹中的冗余动作或退步动作。","importance":"这篇工作不直接改预训练目标，但对 LLM post-train 的训练信号质量有启发。它把 agent 轨迹中的环境交互动作当作基本决策单元，而不是普通 token span。这个视角对工具调用、搜索、代码编辑、网页导航类 RL 很关键：训练信号不能只按最终结果分配，还要区分动作在局部过程中的角色。贡献更偏方法设计和诊断框架，适合泛读。","technical_approach":"方法核心是给每个 environment-facing segment 增加一个语义角色轴。TRIAGE 用结构化 LLM judge 对局部上下文中的动作片段分类，角色包括 decisive progress、useful exploration、no-progress infrastructure、regression。分类器不直接替代 verifier，也不输出自由形式 reward；它只做局部语义诊断。随后用固定的 role-conditioned rule 把角色映射为有界的 segment-level process reward：决定性进展继承较强 outcome-aligned credit，有用探索给有限正向 credit，无进展基础操作压到接近 0，退步动作即使出现在成功轨迹中也被抑制。这样保留 GRPO 的最终 outcome advantage 作为主方向，同时用角色标签修正局部 credit。论文还给出一个理论解释：如果只能观测角色标签，role-conditioned correction 是均方误差意义下的最优片段校正；当固定角色常数与该最优校正对齐时，可以降低 advantage estimation error，并对应更低方差的 policy gradient。","experiments":"实验覆盖 WebShop、Search-QA、ALFWorld 这类需要搜索、点击、导航、对象交互的 agentic 任务。对比项包括标准 GRPO、使用同一 judge 和上下文窗口的标量 LLM process-reward baseline，以及 outcome-supervised shared-backbone value baseline。论文声称 TRIAGE 在这些任务上相对上述基线有稳定收益，并用人工标注片段和角色诊断解释收益来源：失败轨迹中的 useful exploration 不再被全量惩罚，成功轨迹中的 regression 不再被 outcome reward 掩盖。给出的材料没有包含具体提升幅度和完整表格数字，因此不宜外推其规模。","reading_guide":"建议用 20 到 30 分钟泛读。重点看 Introduction 对 多提供信息”。如果关注 agent RL recipe，可进一步看人工标注诊断，判断角色分类是否稳定、是否依赖 judge 偏好。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"TRIAGE addresses credit assignment in agentic RL. A final verifier can judge whether a trajectory succeeds, but vanilla GRPO assigns the same outcome advantage to all action tokens or segments. This penalizes useful exploration in failed rollouts and reinforces redundant or regressive actions in successful rollouts.","importance":"The paper is more relevant to RL post-training than to base pretraining, but it is useful for understanding training-signal quality in tool-use and environment-interaction settings. Its main contribution is to treat environment-facing segments, such as searches, clicks, edits, and object interactions, as the credit-assignment unit. This matters because outcome-only supervision is too coarse for long agent trajectories.","technical_approach":"TRIAGE adds a semantic role axis on top of the outcome axis. A structured LLM judge classifies each local segment into one of four roles: decisive progress, useful exploration, no-progress infrastructure, or regression. The judge is used as a role classifier, not as an unconstrained reward model. A fixed role-conditioned rule then maps labels to bounded process rewards or penalties. Decisive progress receives strong outcome-aligned credit; useful exploration receives bounded positive credit; infrastructure is damped toward zero; regression is suppressed even when the trajectory eventually succeeds. The final verifier outcome remains the primary optimization direction under GRPO. The paper also gives a theoretical justification: role-conditioned correction is the MSE-optimal segment correction measurable from role labels alone, and aligned role constants can reduce advantage-estimation error.","experiments":"The evaluation covers WebShop, Search-QA, and ALFWorld. Baselines include standard GRPO, a scalar LLM-derived process-reward baseline using the same judge and context window, and an outcome-supervised shared-backbone value baseline. TRIAGE is reported to improve consistently over these baselines. Manual segment labels and role diagnostics are used to explain where the gains come from: preserving useful exploration in failed trajectories and suppressing regressive actions inside successful ones. The provided text does not include exact improvement numbers, so the magnitude should be read from the full tables before drawing strong conclusions.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Read the introduction for the two failure modes of outcome-only credit, then inspect the method section for the four-role taxonomy and the fixed role-to-reward mapping. The most informative experimental comparison is against the scalar process-reward baseline, because it tests whether role typing adds information beyond dense reward scoring. The theory section is worth a light pass for the MSE-optimal correction argument, but the practical value depends more on judge reliability and role-label stability.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"GRPO信用分配的关键改进，角色分段奖励的思路可直接复现用于R1-style推理RL训练，重点看§3的规则设计和正确性证明","en":"Key improvement to GRPO credit assignment; role-based segment reward design is directly reproducible for R1-style reasoning RL training, focus on §3 rule design and correctness proof"},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Yuanda Xu","Zhengze Zhou","Hejian Sang","Xiaomin Li","Jiaxin Zhang","Xinchen Du","Zhipeng Wang","Alborz Geramifard"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["GRPO","reinforcement learning","credit assignment","agentic RL","post-training"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32017","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.32017","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.31748","title":"Addressing Over-Refusal in LLMs with Competing Rewards","abstract":"Safety training on language models often induces over-refusal: improved safety on harmful prompts at the cost of increased refusal on harmless ones. Though this trade-off can be mitigated by training models with reinforcement learning (RL) to reason before answering, it does not remove the underlying problem that reasoning can often be a \"rubber stamp\" for a predetermined response. In this paper, we address the safety-refusal trade-off by rethinking how models are trained to reason about safety.","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-30T14:38:49Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_31748.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是安全后训练里一个很具体、也很顽固的问题：模型越安全，越容易对无害请求也拒答。作者认为，现有“让模型先做安全推理再回答”的RL方法并没有真正解决这个问题，因为推理常常只是给既定拒答结论补一个理由，不能承担“在有害解释与无害解释之间做歧义消解”的功能。更细一点说，现有模型通常落入两种失败模式：要么一开始就回避任何危险解释，直接保守拒答；要么确实探索了危险解释，但最后无法稳定收束到安全回答。论文要解决的核心，就是如何让模型在同一条推理链里既敢于探索潜在有害解释，又能把最终输出控制在安全范围内，从而减少 over-refusal，同时不牺牲抗攻击性。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，它不是直接改 pretrain recipe，但对 pretrain↔post-train 接口有现实意义。第一，它把“安全推理”从一个单一结果监督问题，改写成了一个带有探索—恢复结构的信用分配问题，这和长链推理、test-time exploration、甚至 latent reasoning 的训练信号设计是相通的。第二，它指出 outcome-only reward 在含冲突目标的场景里不够用，必须把奖励拆到过程级别；这个结论对任何希望把多个行为目标压进同一模型的RL后训练都很有参考价值。第三，它给出一个值得注意的现象：允许模型在 reasoning segment 中显式接触不安全思路，反而能提升对模糊提示和 reasoning-targeted attack 的鲁棒性。这说明“压制中间思路”未必是最稳的安全训练方式，训练信号的结构可能比标签本身更关键。贡献量级上，这是一个中等偏上的方法性工作，重点不在通用RL新算法，而在安全推理训练目标的重新拆解与实证验证。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 competing rewards。作者把一条生成轨迹拆成 reasoning segment 和 final answer segment，不再像标准 outcome-reward RL 那样用一个统一奖励去评价整条轨迹，而是给两个片段分别打分，并且这两个分数是有意相互竞争的。对 reasoning segment，模型会因为“更充分地探索有害解释、攻击路径或危险意图”而得到更高奖励；对 final answer segment，模型则因为“在前面已经出现危险探索的条件下，仍然给出安全、拒绝或重定向的回答”而得到奖励。这样训练的目标不是让模型从头到尾都安全保守，而是让它学会在内部推理中处理危险可能性，再在输出端做受控收束。\n\n论文里有几个关键设计点。第一，作者强调 competing objectives 的优化必须匹配。如果 reasoning reward 和 answer reward 的优化强度、采样方式或更新节奏不一致，模型会出现不稳定行为，比如只学会危险探索却丢失安全收束，或者重新退化成保守拒答。第二，作者发现仅靠 dense outcome-level rewards 不足以学会这种冲突目标，因为信用分配太粗，模型不知道该把哪一段行为归因为“好探索”或“好收束”。因此他们引入 process-based rewards，也就是在采样 rollout 上对 segment 级别进行奖励标注。第三，作者不是把 unsafe reasoning 单独放到外部工具链或分支解码里，而是要求模型在一条连续 chain-of-thought 中完成“探索—恢复”。这点和很多把安全控制做成前置分类器或最终拒答器的方法不同，它更接近把安全能力内化为推理动态本身。\n\n从全文表述看，这个方法和 TARS 一类安全RL工作的差别，不只是奖励函数换了个形式，而是训练假设变了：TARS 等方法默认 reasoning 的作用是帮助模型更准确地判断是否拒答；这篇工作则认为 reasoning 应该允许受控的危险探索，否则模型无法真正处理歧义提示。作者还专门分析了 pre-filling 这类直接操纵推理前缀的攻击，说明如果模型从未被训练过“在危险思路中恢复”，那么一旦推理被带偏，最终回答更容易失守。","experiments":"实验主角是 SEAR-1.5B。论文最重要的结果不是绝对分数，而是安全—拒答权衡曲线的位置。作者报告 SEAR 在同尺寸开源模型里取得了最好的 safety-refusal trade-off，并且优于最接近的方法 TARS；同时，尽管只有 1.5B 参数，SEAR 的整体表现可与更大的 TARS-7B 接近。这一点如果成立，说明奖励结构的改造带来的收益不只是小幅调参，而是显著影响了模型如何利用推理。\n\n全文还强调了几类关键实验。第一类是行为分析：作者在第3节先证明现有安全推理模型的 CoT 往往是 rubber stamp，而不是歧义消解工具，这为后面的设计提供了动机。第二类是奖励设计消融：他们比较了单一 outcome reward、dense 但非 process 的奖励、以及真正 segment-level 的 process rewards，结论是只有后者能稳定学到“危险探索 + 安全回答”的组合行为。第三类是优化稳定性分析：如果 competing rewards 的优化不匹配，训练会出现明显不稳定。第四类是鲁棒性评测：SEAR 对 adversarial attacks 更稳，尤其对 pre-filling 这类直接诱导模型沿着危险推理继续生成的攻击更有抵抗力。论文摘要截断了部分具体数字，给出的明确信息包括模型规模 1.5B，以及与 TARS-7B 的对比结论；更细的分数、attack success rate、拒答率变化幅度需要直接看结果表和附录。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先看第1节和第3节。第1节把问题定义得很准：不是单纯提高拒答准确率，而是让模型学会处理歧义。第3节值得重点看，因为作者不是直接上方法，而是先证明现有“安全推理”为什么没起到应有作用，这部分决定了后面方法是否站得住。\n\n然后看第4节，尤其是 4.2、4.3、4.4。这里是全文最有信息量的部分：为什么 competing rewards 会不稳定、为什么 outcome-level reward 不够、为什么必须做 process-based credit assignment。读这几节时，重点不是记公式，而是看作者如何把“探索”和“收束”拆成两个可训练目标。\n\n实验部分建议优先看 safety-refusal trade-off 主结果、奖励消融、以及 pre-filling attack 的鲁棒性结果。如果时间有限，30–45 分钟可以抓住主线；如果要判断这套方法能否迁移到更大模型或别的后训练目标，建议连附录一起看，重点关注奖励标注方式、rollout 构造、以及是否存在通过显式 unsafe CoT 泄漏风险的控制措施。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a specific failure mode in safety post-training: improving safety often increases refusal on benign prompts. The authors argue that current “reason-before-answering” RL approaches do not really solve this trade-off, because the reasoning trace often acts as a post-hoc justification for a decision that was already fixed. In practice, existing models tend to fail in two brittle ways: they either avoid unsafe interpretations altogether and default to refusal, or they explore unsafe interpretations but fail to recover to a safe final answer. The core problem is therefore not just refusal calibration, but training a model to use reasoning for ambiguity resolution: it should inspect potentially harmful interpretations when needed, yet still end in a safe response.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it matters for the pretrain–post-train interface. First, it reframes safety reasoning as a credit-assignment problem with exploration and recovery, which is closely related to how one might train long-horizon reasoning or other process-sensitive behaviors. Second, it shows that outcome-only rewards are inadequate when the desired behavior contains internally competing objectives; process-level supervision becomes necessary. That lesson generalizes beyond safety. Third, the paper presents an important empirical claim: allowing unsafe exploration inside the reasoning segment can improve both selectivity on ambiguous prompts and robustness to attacks that manipulate the reasoning process. The contribution is method-oriented rather than algorithmically broad, but the training signal design is substantive and likely useful for anyone thinking about RL-based behavior shaping.","technical_approach":"$1c","experiments":"$1d","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the introduction and the analysis section that diagnoses why current safety reasoning fails. Those sections contain the main technical content.\n\nIn the experiments, prioritize the safety–refusal trade-off plots, the reward ablations, and the robustness results on pre-filling attacks. If time is limited, 30 to 45 minutes is enough to understand the main claim and the method. pena","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-30T14:29:48Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_31732.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"视觉到代码（图表/矢量图/SVG/网页）要求像素级结构对齐与可执行性，SFT Extractor，对生成代码做解析，抽取离散的视觉属性（如颜色、坐标轴范围、元素属性等），再与目标属性对齐计算 element-level reward。动机是：CLIP 过于全局语义，无法惩罚标签/数据点等细节幻觉；像素损失又被字体抗锯齿、padding 等高频渲染差异放大。符号属性奖励试图在“结构正确”与“渲染噪声”之间找到稳定梯度。\n2) Reference-Guided Code webpages) requires executable code whose rendered output matches the input with high structural fidelity. SFT typically learns coarse correspondences but stalls on fine-grained alignment (coordinates, attributes). RL is appealing but fails in practice due to (1) reward coarseness/misalignment (CLIP-like metrics ignore element-level errors; pixel losses are dominated by rendering artifacts) and (2) exploration stagnation in a sparse, brittle code space where early rollouts are often non-executable, collapsing learning signals. The paper aims to make RL workable for this domain.","importance":"More relevant to post-training (RL for multimodal codegen) than to core pretraining, but it offers reusable patterns for “RL feedback that can actually train”: convert the objective into discrete, verifiable symbolic attributes for dense rewards, and use reference injection to avoid cold-start collapse. If you are exploring RL signals that feed back into mid-training/continual training, this is a concrete recipe: (a) discretize what you can verify, (b) reward at the right granularity, (c) ensure early executable trajectories exist.","technical_approach":"UniCoder combines two mechanisms:\n1) Symbolic Attribute Alignment: a lightweight auxiliary LLM acts as an attribute extractor that parses generated code and outputs discrete visual attributes (e.g., colors, axis limits, element properties). Rewards are computed by aligning these attributes with target attributes, aiming to decouple structural intent from pixel-level rendering noise.\n2) Reference-Guided Code Optimization: when performance is low, ground-truth reference code is injected into the rollout buffer to bootstrap exploration, turning early training from blind search into guided optimization (imitation-like anchors plus policy optimization, implemented with GRPO-style updates).\nThe excerpt indicates unified multi-task RL across several benchmarks; detailed implementation knobs (reward decomposition/weights, injection triggers/ratios, execution constraints, renderer specifics) are not visible in the provided text.","experiments":"Confirmed from the excerpt: an 8B model; evaluated on ChartMimic, UniSVG, Design2Code, and ScreenBench; reported as best among open-source models and claimed to narrow the gap to proprietary systems (the text mentions GPT-5). Empirically, SAIL has demonstrated strong performance on this task. However, a formal analysis of its convergence properties has been lacking. We identify a key theoretical challenge: the standard SAIL objective function is not guaranteed to be strongly concave due to unfavorable properties of its Hessian. To address this limitation, we","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-30T11:36:41Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_31524.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作研究 SAIL 这类 self-improving online LLM alignment 算法是否有可证明的收敛性质。SAIL 把在线偏好对齐中的双层问题化为单层 surrogate，但原始目标的 Hessian 曲率不稳定，不能保证强凹性，因此很难给出全局收敛结论。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接作用不在训练基座模型，而在 Pretrain ↔ Posttrain 接口。在线 RLHF/RLAIF 会不断用当前策略采样新数据，数据分布和优化目标同时变化，容易产生分布漂移和训练不稳定。该工作给 SAIL 加入反向 KL 正则，并在受限假设下证明 PL 条件和非渐近收敛，为理解在线对齐中的稳定性提供了一个较清晰的理论框架。价值主要是分析性，而不是新的实用 recipe；假设包括 log-linear policy、有限参数域、Fisher 条件等，离全参数 LLM 训练仍有距离。","technical_approach":"方法称为 SAIL-RevKL。核心修改是在 SAIL surrogate 中加入 reverse KL penalty，用来改善优化几何。作者先指出未正则化 SAIL 目标只在满足曲率阈值时局部强凹，无法直接支撑全局一阶优化保证。加入 RevKL 后，在以 SFT 参数 θ0 为中心、半径 Bθ 的有界凸集合 Θ 内，目标满足更好的几何性质，可建立强凹性或 Polyak–Łojasiewicz 条件。优化算法是 projected stochastic gradient ascent：每步在有界参数集内做随机梯度上升并投影回 Θ。理论分析采用 log-linear policy，特征映射 ψ(s,a) 有界，策略从 SFT policy πθ0 初始化，并假设 Fisher 矩阵具有正定下界。这样的设定接近最后一层线性头更新，而不是完整 LLM 微调；作者也把这个选择解释为降低 feature distortion 和提高分布外稳定性的一种建模方式。","experiments":"理论结果给出正则化目标 gap 的全局线性收敛，并在合适步长和 batch size 下达到 ε 精度的样本复杂度为 \\tilde{O}(ε^{-1} log(ε^{-1}))。实证部分覆盖 continuous-control benchmark 和 LLM alignment 任务，并包含两类设置：LoRA fine-tuning 和 last-layer-only experiments。后者与理论中的 log-linear policy class 更接近，用来检验理论条件对应的训练形态。给定内容未提供具体任务名称、模型规模或数值提升，因此不宜把实验结论解读为对大规模在线 RLHF 的直接性能证明。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。优先看问题设定、原始 SAIL Hessian/曲率分析、RevKL 如何导出 PL 条件，以及 projected stochastic gradient ascent 的收敛定理。实验部分重点看 last-layer-only 与 LoRA 设置的差异，因为这决定理论和真实 LLM 对齐之间的距离。若关注工程落地，需要特别检查 RevKL 系数、参数域半径、Fisher 条件等假设是否能在实际 online alignment 中被近似满足。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"This work studies whether self-improving online alignment algorithms such as SAIL have provable convergence guarantees. SAIL turns a bilevel online alignment formulation into a single-level surrogate, but the original objective can have unfavorable Hessian curvature and is not guaranteed to be strongly concave, making global convergence hard to establish.","importance":"The relevance is mainly at the interface between pretraining and post-training. Online RLHF or RLAIF repeatedly samples from the current policy, so the data distribution and the optimization target evolve together. This coupling can create distribution shift and unstable updates. By adding a reverse-KL regularizer and proving a PL-style condition under explicit assumptions, the work provides a cleaner theoretical lens for online alignment stability. The contribution is analytical rather than a directly deployable large-scale recipe. The assumptions, including a log-linear policy class, bounded parameter domain, and Fisher non-degeneracy, remain far from full-parameter LLM training.","technical_approach":"The proposed objective is SAIL-RevKL. The key change is a reverse-KL penalty added to the SAIL surrogate to improve the optimization geometry. The analysis first shows that the unregularized SAIL objective only has local strong concavity under a curvature threshold. With RevKL, the objective satisfies stronger geometry, including strong concavity or a Polyak–Łojasiewicz condition, on a bounded convex domain around the SFT initialization θ0. The optimization method is projected stochastic gradient ascent: stochastic gradient steps are followed by projection back to the feasible set Θ. The theory uses a log-linear policy class with bounded features ψ(s,a), initializes from the SFT policy πθ0, and assumes a positive lower bound on the Fisher matrix. This setup is closest to updating a final linear head while freezing the representation, rather than full LLM fine-tuning.","experiments":"The main theoretical result is global linear convergence of the regularized objective gap, with sample complexity \\tilde{O}(ε^{-1} log(ε^{-1})) for reaching ε accuracy under the stated step-size and batch-size choices. The empirical section includes both continuous-control benchmarks and LLM alignment tasks. It evaluates practical LoRA fine-tuning as well as last-layer-only training, the latter being the closest experimental counterpart to the log-linear policy class used in the proof. The provided content does not include task names, model sizes, or detailed numerical gains, so the experiments should be read as supporting evidence for the theoretical setup rather than a definitive result for large-scale online RLHF.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Its central claim is that the protected object should be the activation subspace used by a capability, not a proxy such as parameter distance, weight singular vectors, gradient history, or output matching.","importance":"The main value for pretraining-oriented work is in continual pretraining and capability-preserving adaptation. Fora gives a concrete mechanism for estimating which directions a preserved capability uses during inference, using only unlabeled calibration inputs. This is more operational than protecting large singular directions of the pretrained weight matrix. The contribution is not a new base pretraining recipe, but it gives useful machinery for measuring and constraining capability drift after pretraining.","technical_approach":"For each linear layer, the method forwards unlabeled examples from the capability to be preserved, collects input activations, computes their covariance, and extracts the top-k eigenvectors Q. These define a right projector P_Q = I - QQ^T, which prevents the update from reading activation directions associated with the preserved capability. The update also uses a left projector P_U = I - U_1 U_1^T, where U_1 comes from dominant output singular directions of the pretrained weight W_0, to avoid writing into major output directions. A narrow spectral channel is kept for bounded plasticity, so the method does not simply freeze all capability-related computation. The low-rank variant replaces the dense residual matrix M with a LoRA-style alpha/r · B A parameterization.","experiments":"Experiments are run on Qwen3-1.7B in three full-rank adaptation settings. A weight-space projection baseline gives 4.86 perplexity at the same adaptation level, close to unprotected full fine-tuning. When learning GSM8K while preserving translation, Fora exactly matches the baseline translation perplexity of 4.35. In the reverse direction, learning translation while preserving math, Fora loses only 0.9 GSM8K points, compared with a 2.3-point drop under full fine-tuning. The key ablation swaps P_V for P_Q and shows that the gain comes from the source of the protected subspace.","reading_guide":"A 25-35 minute skim is enough. represent","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-30T17:43:44Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_32008.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是一个解释性研究里长期被默认、但很少被正面检验的问题：当闭源前沿模型只能通过 API 访问时，研究者能否用开源模型作为 surrogate，去解释闭源模型的行为与内部机制？作者把这个问题形式化为 surrogate fidelity，也就是“在 surrogate 模型上测到的量，能多大程度预测 target 模型上的对应量”。他们特别强调，很多人默认“如果两个模型预测相近，那么解释也大致可迁移”，但全文实验表明这个假设并不稳，尤其在 attribution 层面经常失效。","importance":"它不是预训练方法论文，但对基座研究仍有直接价值。第一，当前很多关于训练动态、表示结构、对齐副作用的结论，都是在开源模型上做白盒分析，再外推到闭源大模型；这篇工作提醒这种外推需要先验证 fidelity。第二，作者提出了一套按访问权限分层的 fidelity 框架，尤其把 top-K logprob 可得的 API 信号利用到 prediction / attribution 测量里，这对闭源模型审计、对齐评估、以及 post-train 行为归因都很实用。第三，论文给出一个很重要的负面结果：prediction fidelity 往往显著高估 attribution fidelity。也就是说，两个模型可以在“答什么”上很像，但在“为什么这么答”上差很多。对任何想把开源模型当作闭源模型代理来做机制分析的人，这都是需要先过的一道检查。贡献量级上，这是一个评估框架加系统性实证工作，方法不复杂，但问题提得准，结论也足够具体。","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"实验覆盖 11 个模型，来自 上的数值，需要直接看实验章节和附录表格。","reading_guide":"泛读建议很明确。先看 Introduction 和 Preliminaries。Introduction 讲清楚了问题边界：不是讨论某个解释工具本身好不好，而是讨论“开源模型能否作为闭源模型解释代理”。Preliminaries 里关于 log-odds direction 的推导值得看，因为这是整篇工作能在 API 的脱钩有多严重；二是同家族不同尺度与跨家族之间，哪类 surrogate 更可靠。\n\n如果只有 25–35 分钟，读完方法定义和主实验就够了；如果要把它用于闭源模型审计或 MI 研究设计，建议再看作者如何实现 leave-one-out attribution、如何处理 API top-K logprob 的限制，以及附录里对 attention 等白盒指标失效的分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a question that is often assumed away in mechanistic interpretability: when a frontier model is only accessible through an API, under what conditions can an open model serve as a faithful surrogate for explaining it? The authors formalize this as surrogate fidelity, meaning how well a measurement made on a surrogate model predicts the corresponding measurement on a target model. The key challenge is that many interpretability claims are implicitly transferred across models without checking whether the transfer is valid at the level of outputs, attributions, or internal representations.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is highly relevant to foundation-model research. A large fraction of current analysis on representations, alignment side effects, and training-induced behaviors is done on open models and then informally generalized to closed frontier systems. This paper argues that such generalization needs an explicit fidelity check. Its main practical contribution is a hierarchy of fidelity metrics aligned with access constraints, including metrics that can be computed from top-K log-probabilities alone. Its main empirical contribution is a strong negative result: prediction fidelity often substantially overstates attribution fidelity. In other words, two models may agree on what answer to give while disagreeing on why. For anyone using open models as proxies for closed ones, that is a serious warning.","technical_approach":"$21","experiments":"$22","reading_guide":"$23","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看 attribution fidelity 和 access-validity inversion 的实验；它会降低用开源模型解释闭源模型的信心。","en":"Read the attribution-fidelity and access-validity-inversion results; they weaken the case for explaining closed models via open surrogates."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Philippe Chlenski","Zachariah Carmichael","Ayush Warikoo","Chia-Tse Shao","Yingxiao Ye","Aobo Yang","Vivek Miglani","Nehal Bandi"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["mechanistic-interpretability","surrogate-models","closed-models","attribution","logprobs"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32008","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.32008","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.30815","title":"When transformers learn \"impossible\" languages, what do they learn?","abstract":"Recent work suggests that transformer language models show a bias towards human languages over unnatural (\"impossible\") languages argued to be unacquirable by humans. However, this literature has largely based these claims on differences in sample efficiency and test-set perplexity, rather than on direct evaluations of the linguistic capacities that could plausibly explain non-attestation in human languages. We evaluate two theoretically motivated linking hypotheses: impossibility arising from d","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-29T18:42:03Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_30815.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作问的是：Transformer 在训练于“人类语言中不出现、被认为难以习得”的 impossible language 时，到底学到了什么。它不满足于比较 perplexity 或 sample efficiency，而是把问题拆成两个能力：对语法性的被动敏感性，以及生成合格句子的能力。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的启发在于，它把语言建模损失和语言能力之间的关系做了更细的拆分。较高 perplexity 不必然表示模型没有学到语法判断能力；生成质量也不一定和 held-out perplexity 或局部信息熵完全一致。这对解释预训练动态、评测 synthetic language、以及用 LM 研究归纳偏置都有参考价值，但它不是直接改进大模型训练 recipe 的工作。","technical_approach":"方法是训练 GPT-2 风格语言模型，语料来自 BabyLM English 及其一系列扰动版本。扰动包括 FullReverse、PartialReverse、LocalShuffle3/5/10、EvenOddShuffle、Deterministic Shuffle、Non-deterministic Shuffle 等，目标是构造线性顺序异常、局部性不同的语言变体。语法敏感性用 BLiMP minimal pairs 评估，并把 BLiMP 样本按同样规则转换到对应 impossible language。生成能力则利用这些扰动语言可逆的性质：先让模型生成 impossible language 句子，再映射回 English，用 LLM 评估其句子质量。分析中还引入 m-local entropy，用来描述信息局部性，并和 perplexity 一起解释学习难度。","experiments":"主要结果有两点。第一，模型在 impossible language 上仍能获得相当程度的被动语法敏感性，BLiMP 表现不是断崖式失败，而是随 test perplexity 和 m-local entropy 逐步下降。第二，生成能力下降更明显：这些模型生成的合格句子明显更少，而且这种下降并不严格跟 held-out perplexity 或 m-local entropy 对齐。这个差异说明，minimal-pair 判断、分布建模损失和自由生成质量测到的是不同能力。给出的全文片段没有包含完整数值表，因此不应过度解读具体幅度。","reading_guide":"泛读 25–35 分钟。先看扰动语言定义表，确认每种 impossible language 改变了什么统计结构。然后看 BLiMP 构造方式，因为这是语法敏感性结论是否可信的关键。生成评估部分也建议读，尤其是“生成后反转为 English 再用 LLM 评价”的设计，它比只看 perplexity 更接近 transmission/generation 假设。若只关心预训练动态，重点看 perplexity、m-local entropy 与 BLiMP/生成质量之间的不一致。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work asks what Transformer language models actually learn when trained on perturbed, supposedly “impossible” variants of English. Instead of relying only on perplexity or sample efficiency, it separates two capacities: passive sensitivity to grammatical well-formedness and the ability to generate well-formed sentences.","importance":"The contribution is mainly interpretive for pretraining research. It shows that language modeling loss, grammatical sensitivity, and open-ended generation quality can diverge. Higher perplexity on an artificial language does not necessarily mean the model failed to learn grammatical contrasts, and generation failure is not fully predicted by held-out perplexity or local information measures. This is useful for studying inductive bias, synthetic language pretraining, and the limits of perplexity as a proxy for linguistic competence.","technical_approach":"The authors train GPT-2-style models on the English BabyLM corpus and several perturbed versions of English. The perturbations include FullReverse, PartialReverse, LocalShuffle with different window sizes, EvenOddShuffle, deterministic shuffle, and non-deterministic shuffle. For grammatical sensitivity, they transform BLiMP minimal pairs using the same perturbation rules and test whether the models prefer grammatical over ungrammatical variants. For generation, they exploit the invertibility of the constructed languages: model outputs in an impossible language are mapped back to English and then judged for quality with an LLM. The analysis also uses m-local entropy as a measure of information locality, alongside held-out perplexity.","experiments":"The main empirical pattern is that models trained on impossible languages retain substantial passive grammatical sensitivity. Performance degrades gradually rather than collapsing, and the degradation is related to test-set perplexity and m-local entropy. In contrast, generative performance is much weaker: models trained on the perturbed languages produce fewer well-formed sentences, and this failure does not align cleanly with held-out perplexity or m-local entropy. The provided full-text excerpt does not include the complete numerical tables, so the robust takeaway is the qualitative dissociation among perplexity, minimal-pair sensitivity, and free generation.","reading_guide":"A focused skim should take 25–35 minutes. This is only well posed after fixing the model's residual-stream gauge, which we show is architecture-dependent: LayerNorm residual charts have permutation gauge $S_d$ (up to a global sign flip), while RMSNorm charts with generic per-channel gain have signed-permutation gauge $B_d = S_d \\ltimes \\{\\pm 1\\}^d$. Permutation-only","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-30T17:02:33Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_31963.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个常被忽略、但对坐标级分析和工具迁移很关键的问题：RMSNorm Transformer 的残差流坐标并不只有“置换”等价，而是存在“带符号置换” gauge。若只按 permutation 去对齐两个 checkpoint、两个 seed、或训练轨迹上的不同时刻，就会把很多本来等价的坐标当成不匹配，导致 steering vector、SAE feature、LoRA 更新、merge 对齐、甚至 AdamW optimizer state 的跨模型搬运失真。论文要解决的不是功能等价下的任意重参数化，而是“保留坐标身份”的离散对称群到底是什么，以及如何从激活中把这个正确的 gauge 恢复出来。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要在两个层面。第一，它把 RMSNorm 模型里一个很基础的对称性问题讲清楚了：LayerNorm 残差流天然对应的是 S_d（外加全局符号），而 RMSNorm 在 generic per-channel gain 下对应的是 B_d = S_d ⋉ {±1}^d。这个结论直接影响所有依赖“第 i 个坐标/神经元/特征”可比性的工作。第二，它给出的是可操作的 transport 规则，而不是纯理论提醒。现在很多开源 LLM 都是 RMSNorm，若继续沿用 permutation-only 的 neuron matching 或 merge alignment，很多坐标级结论会系统性失真。对做 interpretability、checkpoint surgery、model merging、continual finetune 恢复、optimizer state 迁移的人，这篇文章提供了一个必要的校正。","technical_approach":"技术上分三步。第一步是群论和参数化层面的刻画。作者证明：在保留原生坐标语义、显式保留 RMSNorm gain γ 的参数化下，RMSNorm 残差流允许的最大 coordinate-preserving 离散对称群是 hyperoctahedral group B_d，也就是任意坐标置换加每个坐标独立翻转符号。若把 γ 吸收到相邻线性层里，可以恢复更宽的正交自由度，但那已经改变了坐标系，不再适合讨论稀疏坐标身份、top-k neuron、SAE dictionary 这类对象。第二步是 gauge recovery。作者把传统 activation matching 的 Hungarian assignment 改成 sign-marginalized 版本：匹配代价不再用 signed correlation，而是用绝对相关 |<h_i, h'_j>|，匹配完成后再由相关符号恢复 sign。关键点在于，正确匹配在 RMSNorm 下可能是强负相关；若直接最大化 signed correlation，负号那一半真匹配会被系统性排斥。文中给了 decorrelated source coordinates 下的 sharp population failure mode：当一半真实符号为负时，即使 probe 无限多，signed-correlation matching 也会稳定失败。第三步是 transport via atlas。与其只做两个端点的全局匹配，作者沿训练轨迹保存局部 B_d gauge，并把这些局部高置信小步匹配复合起来，形成 transport-via-base。这样做利用了训练相邻 checkpoint 之间变化小、局部 margin 高的事实，比直接 endpoint matching 更容易恢复真实坐标对应。论文还把同样的 signed transport 规则扩展到 merge 和 AdamW state 恢复，强调 READ/WRITE 都要 obey B_d-covariance。","experiments":"实验重点不是大规模 benchmark，而是验证“少了 sign 会怎样坏”。几个结果比较关键。1）在记录了真实 gauge 的 LLM activation 上，signed-cost recovery 基本停留在约 50%，而 sign-marginalized recovery 可以到 100%，直接对应理论里的负相关失配。2）在一个 gauge-instrumented 的 Qwen2.5-1.5B finetuning 轨迹上，训练 1500 steps，并在 step 0 施加随机 B_d gauge 作为真值。端点直接匹配只能恢复 60.3% 坐标；transport-via-base 提升到 91.1%；若只是 endpoint-via-base 但不利用局部链式 transport，则只有 11.4%。这说明收益来自“很多局部小步高 margin 匹配”的复合，而不是简单拿 base 当参考。3）在 Llama-2-7B 的 gauge-scramble merge 测试里，signed alignment 把 peak-above-chord barrier 从 6.14 降到 0.00；精确 permutation-only alignment 反而有害。4）在独立训练的 10M RMSNorm Transformer 不同 seed 间，也观察到自然出现的 sign mismatch；signed alignment 相比 permutation-only，把 merge barrier 从 0.66 降到 0.42。5）对 optimizer state，signed transport 可以保持 resumed trajectory，而 permutation-only 即便从函数等价 checkpoint 出发也会训练发散或偏离。文中还给出一个理论 corollary：若对 RMSNorm 更新误用 permutation-only，对 learned weight update 引入的期望平方误差量级是 2||ΔW||_F^2，和更新本身能量同阶，这解释了为什么错误不是“小噪声”，而是结构性破坏。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三件事。第一，看引言和理论部分里对 LayerNorm 与 RMSNorm gauge 的区分，尤其是 Theorem population 例子里失败是如何必然发生的。第三，看训练轨迹 transport 的实验图表，尤其 Qwen2.5-1.5B 1500-step 那组结果，它最能说明 atlas 思路的实际价值。若时间有限，优先读 Introduction、理论结论、activation recovery 实验、transport-via-base 实验和 merge/optimizer state parameterization，哪些对称性仍然保留坐标身份。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a precise but underappreciated issue in RMSNorm transformers: coordinate-indexed objects are not well-defined up to permutations alone. In RMSNorm residual streams, the native coordinate-preserving gauge is a signed permutation group, not just S_d. If one aligns checkpoints, seeds, or training-time states using permutation-only matching, many coordinates that are actually equivalent under a sign flip will be treated as mismatches. This breaks the transport of neuron-level artifacts such as steering vectors, SAE features, sparse neuron sets, LoRA updates, merge alignments, and even optimizer states.","importance":"Its value for pretraining-oriented work is not that it improves perplexity, but that it fixes a foundational assumption behind a growing set of workflows around open-weight LLMs. Many modern models use RMSNorm, yet much of the alignment tooling inherited from LayerNorm-era work still assumes permutation symmetry only. The paper shows that this is symmetry-incomplete for RMSNorm and can induce errors on the same scale as the update being transported. For interpretability, checkpoint surgery, model merging, continual finetuning, and optimizer-state recovery, this is a necessary correction rather than a cosmetic refinement.","technical_approach":"$24","experiments":"$25","reading_guide":"For a skim, focus on three parts. 59","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-30T07:33:18Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2606_31257.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要纠正一个在 probe-and-steer 统一解释成“latent knowledge not deployed”的现象，拆成三类机制：真实 grounded 且方向正确、真实 grounded 但符号反了、以及完全由 prior 驱动。对做 VLM 预训练、表征分析、representation engineering、latent capability recovery 的人，这个结论很直接：只看可解码性和可操控性不够，必须显式检查信息是否依赖视觉输入。否则很容易把“可读出”误当成“已 grounding”，把“可恢复”误当成“视觉能力被释放”。","technical_approach":"方法上有三层。第一层是任务分解。作者选用 ViewSpatial-Bench 里的 camera-relative direction 子集，把问题拆成 horizontal、vertical、depth 三个纯轴向子任务，并区分 18 类原始准确率和 axis 内二分类准确率。这个设计很关键，因为它让“哪一维出了问题”可以被单独定位，而不是被混合空间关系掩盖。第二层是标准的 probe + steering 管线。作者在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 的 decision token residual stream 上做五折交叉验证 logistic probe，先 PCA 到 50 维，再逐层读出每个轴的方向；随后用 diff-of-means 或 probe weight 回投得到方向，在 L20-L24 这一带做无训练 projection/activation edit，用单一全局系数 gamma 放大 top-k 神经元上的局部方向。按传统解释，这一步如果能显著提升行为，就会被视为“模型内部已有该知识，只是没部署出来”。第三层才是论文真正的核心：vision-ablation arbiter。它不改 prompt、不改选项、不改解码，只把图像换成同尺寸灰图，再比较真实图像与灰图条件下的基线准确率和 steering 后增益。作者据此定义三种 grounding regime：如果真实图像显著优于灰图，且灰图退到机会水平，说明该轴是 grounded-correct；如果真实图像低于机会水平，而灰图回到机会附近，说明模型在看图，但把视觉 cue 的符号用反了，即 grounded-inverted；如果真实图像和灰图几乎一致，且 steering 增益在两种条件下也一致，则说明 probe/steering 读到并放大的只是 prior，而非视觉信息。作者还做了多种稳健性检查，避免“灰图本身是异常输入”这个反驳，包括其他形式的视觉消融和 in-distribution 但语义不匹配的真实图像替换。全文后半部分进一步分析 depth inversion 的几何来源，并尝试最小编辑修正：在表征最集中的模型上，训练自由的 rotation 就能纠正；在表征更分散的模型上，需要训练式低秩编辑。这一点把“错误是局部几何错位还是分布式表征问题”也区分开了。","experiments":"最关键的结果集中在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 的三轴对比。基线行为上，horizontal 表现较强，vertical 接近随机，depth 低于随机。probe 却给出相反印象：三个轴都能从 residual stream 解出来，vertical 甚至最好，文中给出的 within-axis probe 准确率大致是 vertical 94%、horizontal 85%，整体范围在 73% 到 97% 之间。更诱人的是无训练 projection：vertical 轴的行为准确率能从 59% 提到 79%，而 horizontal 基本不受影响，这正是“latent knowledge 被解锁”的典型信号。但灰图仲裁一做，解释完全变了。horizontal 在真实图像下约 82%，灰图掉到 49%，说明它确实依赖视觉；depth 在真实图像下只有 31%，灰图回到 45%，说明错误恰恰来自看图；vertical 则在真实图像和灰图下几乎一样，59% 对 60%，而 projection 增益也几乎一样，盲图 +19、真实图 +21，说明被放大的不是视觉表征，而是一个“默认 above”的语言先验。泛化实验覆盖 14 个 VLM、6 个 LM family、2B 到 27B 参数。作者报告的总体模式是：能力较强的模型里，horizontal 往往 grounded-correct，vertical 多数 prior-driven，depth inversion 会随规模在家族内部出现。另一个重要结果是任务边界：同一批模型在 camera-egocentric depth 上会 inverted，但在 near-field depth 和 metric depth 上又能 grounded-correct，说明问题不是“模型完全不懂深度”，而是某一类相机坐标语义映射出了系统性符号错误。修正实验里，7/8 个模型、5 个 family 复现了 depth inversion；最干净的模型可用训练自由 rotation 修正，效果接近训练式编辑，而更分布式的模型则需要低秩训练编辑，形成一个按 correction complexity 排列的谱系。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但建议把引言、4 节、5 节、6 节连着看。引言已经把最重要的反常现象和三类 regime 讲清楚。4 节要重点看三轴在 probe、基线行为、灰图控制、projection 增益上的并列表格或图，那里能直接看到“最可解码的轴反而最不 grounded”。如果只看一张图，优先看真实图像与灰图条件并排的结果图。5 节值得快速扫一遍，因为它回答了两个常见质疑：这是不是 Qwen 单模型偶然现象，以及灰图是不是 out-of-distribution artifact。6 节如果关心表征编辑和机制解释，可以细看。那里讨论了 depth inversion 是不是一个可由简单几何旋转修正的局部错位，以及为什么有些模型需要训练式低秩编辑。阅读时要抓住一个主线：作者不是在否定 probe 或 steering，而是在给它们加一个必要的因果判别步骤。预计 25 到 35 分钟能抓住主要结论；如果要复现实验，优先关注 setup、arbiter 定义、以及附录里的消融细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper challenges a common inference in VLM interpretability: if a target attribute is linearly decodable from hidden states, and a training-free steering/projection along that direction improves behavior, does that mean the model already has the visual knowledge but fails to deploy it? The answer here is no. Using camera-relative spatial reasoning as a controlled case, the authors show that decodability and steerability can both be high even when the behavior is not grounded in the image at all. They also uncover a second failure mode that is easy to miss: the model may genuinely use visual evidence, but with the wrong sign, so looking at the image makes it worse than chance.","importance":"The main value is methodological. The paper introduces a very cheap causal control—replace the image with a same-size gray blank and rerun the exact same prompt and decoding—that separates three mechanisms that probing and steering otherwise collapse into one story. For pretraining and representation-analysis work on VLMs, this matters because many claims about latent capability recovery rely on the chain “decodable therefore represented, steerable therefore recoverable.” This study shows that such claims are incomplete unless they test whether the recovered behavior actually depends on vision. In practice, the proposed arbiter is simple enough to become a default control for probe-based and representation-engineering results in multimodal models.","technical_approach":"$29","experiments":"$2a","reading_guide":"For a skim-level read, focus on the introduction and the sections that compare probe accuracy, baseline behavior, and gray-image controls side by side. Those pages contain the central lesson: the most decodable axis can be the least grounded. The cross-model section is worth a quick pass because it answers two obvious questions—whether the phenomenon is specific to one model, and whether the blank-image control is just an out-of-distribution artifact. If you care about mechanistic editing, spend extra time on the correction section, where the paper distinguishes simple geometric misalignment from more distributed representational errors. A practical reading strategy is to track one question throughout: when behavior improves after steering, is the model using more vision, or just using its prior more strongly? Around 25–35 minutes should be enough to extract the main claims and the experimental logic.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看 blank-image arbiter 和三类 grounding taxonomy；它会降低你对 VLM probe/steering 结果的信任。","en":"Read the blank-image arbiter and the three-regime taxonomy; it should make you less trusting of VLM probe-and-steer claims."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Chih-Ting Liao","Fei Shen","Xin Cao","Tat-Seng Chua"],"notable_authors":["Tat-Seng Chua"],"tags":["vlm-grounding","mechanistic-analysis","linear-probing","causal-ablation","spatial-reasoning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31257","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.31257","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.32000","title":"Radial Suppression Accelerates Algorithmic Generalization: A Geometric Analysis of Delayed Generalization","abstract":"Why do neural networks memorize algorithmic training data long before they generalize?
